review_text: The food is amazing 🤩 and the price was cheap. The bathroom could have felt a little cleaner but the place is busy. The decorations were great and a lot of little kids were there with the families and they seemed to really like it so I would say it's a good family atmosphere. The bar was nice and had plenty of drinks to choose from. The salsa is spicy I loved it. I even paid 53¢ for extra spicy sauce. I had taken it out, so I carried it back to the room. I had the deluxe nachos with shredded chicken and refried beans. They put everything in separate containers so it wouldn't get soggy. I thought that was thoughtful.
